This motif is found in pyruvate:ferredoxin oxidoreductase (POR) and related enzymes.<br><br>- **&quot;IGKIYGG&quot; motif**: Contains the conserved lysine involved in thiamine pyrophosphate (TPP) binding, characteristic of the OOR family.<br><br>- **&quot;GSPKG&quot; motif**: Another conserved element in the TPP-binding domain of 2-oxoacid oxidoreductases.<br><br>Now evaluating each option:<br><br>- **Option A**: NAD⁺-dependent oxidative decarboxylation would describe enzymes like branched-chain α-keto acid dehydrogenase, which uses NAD⁺ as electron acceptor. The motifs here don&#x27;t match that family.<br><br>- **Option B**: Reductive amination describes amino acid dehydrogenases (e.g., glutamate dehydrogenase), which have completely different structural features.<br><br>- **Option C**: Acyl-CoA hydrolysis describes thioesterases, which lack TPP-binding motifs.<br><br>- **Option D**: CoA-dependent oxidative decarboxylation with ferredoxin as electron acceptor perfectly matches the **2-oxoacid:ferredoxin oxidoreductase** family.
